1b1(dkon brtsegs): @#//rgya gar skad du/Ar+ya sid ha pa ra prid tsha/nA ma ma hA yA na su trA/

2a1(dkon brtsegs): @#//mdun du bdar te lhan cig tu//rgyal po'i khab nas de byung nas//rgyal po'i 'byor ba chen po yis//gser las byas pa'i gdugs thogs te//rdzogs sangs 
2a2(dkon brtsegs): rgyas kyi gan song nas//zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal te//lnga brgya po'i gdugs de dag/tshang par thub pa che la phul//kun kyang byang chub don du zhugs//rdzogs 
2a3(dkon brtsegs): sangs rgyas kyi mthu yis ni//de dag mi 'jigs skyes gyur nas//seng ge thal mo sbyar byas te//rdzogs sangs rgyas la zhus pa ni//byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa gang //
2a4(dkon brtsegs): de ni 'dren pas bdag la gsungs//gang gis mdzes par 'gyur ba lags//gang gis brdzus te skye par 'gyur//skye ba dran par ji ltar 'gyur//thub pa chen po lung bstan 
2a5(dkon brtsegs): gsol//de dag lhag bsam ye shes kyis//mkhyen nas sangs rgyas lung bstan pa//byang chub sems dpa'i sbyod gang zhes//khyod kyis dris pa de bstan to//bzod pa yis ni 
2a6(dkon brtsegs): mdzes par 'gyur//tshangs par spyod pas rdzus te skye//chos kyi sbyin pas skye ba dran//gzhon nu de ltar shes par gyis//gang gis sum cu rtsa gnyis mtshan//dpe byad bzang po 
2a7(dkon brtsegs): brgyad cu dang //gser mdog 'dra ba'i lus 'gyur zhing //de mthong sems can dga' bar 'gyur//sbyin pa kun kyis mtshan tu 'gyur//byams pas de bzhin dpe byad bzang //
2a8(dkon brtsegs): sems can kun la sems mnyam pas//rgyal ba gser gyi mdog 'drar 'gyur//gzungs ni gang gis thob par 'gyur//gang gis ting 'dzin skye 'gyur lags//gzung ba'i tshig 
2b1(dkon brtsegs): du gang gis 'gyur//thub pa chen po bstan tu gsol//chos la bzod pas gzungs rnams 'thob//sems bsgoms pas ni ting 'dzin skye//rgyal ba'i tshig rnams spyad byas na//gzung bar 'os ba'i tshig tu 
2b2(dkon brtsegs): 'gyur/dran dang ldan par ji ltar 'gyur//thub pa ji ltar blo gros ldan//ji ltar chos spyod 'gyur ba lags//skyes bu dam pa mkhas cis 'gyur//g.yo med pas ni dran ldan 'gyur//don la sems pa 
2b3(dkon brtsegs): blo gros ldan//gus dang bcas pas chos spyod 'gyur//chos kyi mtshan nyid rtogs pa mkhas//'jig rten mgon po'i spyan sngar ni//las gang gis ni skye bar 'gyur//'jig rten mgon gyi mngon sum du//
2b4(dkon brtsegs): zhu bzhu bar ji ltar 'gyur//gang zhig chos kyi sbyin pa la//nam yang bar chad mi bye ba//de ni des na myur rab du//'jig rten mgon dang phrad par 'gyur//gang gis mi khom spong bar 'gyur//gang gis 
2b5(dkon brtsegs): bzang 'gror mchi bar 'gyur//skye ba kun tu skye ba na//bag mchis par ni ji ltar 'gyur//dad pas mi khom spong bar 'gyur //tshul khrims kyis ni bzang 'gror 'gro//stong pa nyid ni bsgom byas na//
2b6(dkon brtsegs): rtag tu bag dang ldan par 'gyur//thub pa byang chub sems dpa' rnams//ji ltar las rnams dag par 'gyur//ji ltar bdud kyi snyis mi thogs//ji ltar chos kyis 'phongs 'gyur spong //rnam 
2b7(dkon brtsegs): par grol bas las rnams bdag/brtson 'grus kyis ni bdud 'dul 'gyur//ci smras de bzhin byas pas ni//chos kyis 'phongs pa spong bar 'gyur//ka la bing ka'i sgra snyan dang //tshangs 
2b8(dkon brtsegs): pa'i dbyangs 'drar ji ltar 'gyur//thub pa mos pa sna tshogs kyi//sems can rnams ni gang gis dga'//bden par smras pas tshangs pa'i dbyangs//ngag rtsub spangs pas sgra snyan 'gyur//kyal 
3a1(dkon brtsegs): ba'i tshig dang phra ma rnams//spangs pas sems can dga' bar 'gyur//ji ltar tshe ring 'gyur ba lags//bro nad nyung bar ji ltar 'gyur//rnam par 'dren pa gang gis na//g.yog 
3a2(dkon brtsegs): 'khor rnams ni mi phyed 'gyur//gsod pa mi byed tshe ring 'gyur//mi 'tshe bas ni nad nyung 'gyur//mi 'thun pa dag sdums byas na//g.yog 'khor rnams ni mi phyed 'byur//don rnams ji ltar 'grub 'gyur 
3a3(dkon brtsegs): lags//gang gis longs spyod mi nyams 'gyur//gang dang gang du skye ba der//dbang chen grags par ji ltar 'gyur//chags pa med pas don rnams 'grub//ser sna med pas longs spyod 'phel//
3a4(dkon brtsegs): gang dang gang du skyes pa der//phrag dog med pas dbang cher grags//ji ltar lha'i mig 'thob 'gyur//lha'i rna ba ji ltar 'thob//sems can 'chi 'pho skye ba dag/'dren pa gang gis 
3a5(dkon brtsegs): 'tshal bar 'gyur//mar me byin pas mig 'thob 'gyur//rol mo'i sgra yis rna ba 'dag/'chi 'pho skye shes ye shes kyis//de dag de ltar 'grub par 'gyur//'dren pa byang chub sems 
3a6(dkon brtsegs): dpa' yi//zag pa gang gis bas par 'gyur//tshe rabs snga dran cis 'gyur lags//rdzu 'phrul ci yis thob par 'gyur//bzhon pa byin pas rdzu 'phrul 'thob//dran pa bsgoms pas 
3a7(dkon brtsegs): rjes su dran//mtha' gnyis rab du spang byas na//zag pa zad par reg par 'gyur//gang gis zhing ni phun sum tshogs//cis ni dge 'dun phun sum tshogs//gang gis phun sum tshogs 
3a8(dkon brtsegs): 'od 'gyur//gang gis phyogs bcur lhang nger gda'//smon lam gyis ni phun sum zhing //bsdu bas dge 'dun phun sum tshogs //'dir ni rin chen gdugs phul bas//phyogs bcur lhang 
3b1(dkon brtsegs): der snang bar 'gyur//chos rnams thams cad bsdu ba ni//chos gang gis ni thob par 'gyur//gang dang gang du skye ba der//byang chub sems dpa' dga' ba 'thob//sems can thams cad thar bya'i phyir//
3b2(dkon brtsegs): byang chub don du sems btud pa//des ni chos rnams bsdu ba 'thob//der ni dga' ba'ang thob par 'gyur//bslab pa ci 'dra bslabs pa dang //sems ni ci 'dra bsgoms pa yis//thub pa chen po 
3b3(dkon brtsegs): khyod 'dra ba'i//ston pa thams cad mkhyen par 'gyur//sems can ma lus thams cad la//mnyam pa'i sems nyid bslabs pa dang //mnyam par sems ni bsgoms pas na//thams cad zil 
3b4(dkon brtsegs): gnon rgyal bar 'gyur//skye ba kun tu byang chub sems//rmi lam na yang sems de ni//'dor mi 'gyur na ma nyal tshe//smos ci 'tshal bar gang gis 'gyur//grong dang grong khyer rnams su 
3b5(dkon brtsegs): 'am//gang du gnas pa'i yul rnams su//byang chub la ni yang dag 'dzud//des na sems 'dor mi 'gyur ro//gang dang gang du skye bar yang //ji ltar stobs ni cher 'gyur zhing //rtag tu 
3b6(dkon brtsegs): mthu yis 'phags pa dang //bdud rnams bye bas mi tshugs 'gyur//'dir ni snying po lnga byin zhing //skrag la mi 'jigs byin ni na//gang dang gang du skyes par yang //des ni stobs ni cher 
3b7(dkon brtsegs): 'gyur te//rtag tu mthu yis 'phags pa dang //bdud rnams bye bas mi tshul so//byang chub sems dpa' spyod pa de//'jig rten mgon gyi mngon sum du//lung bstan pa 'di thos nas 
3b8(dkon brtsegs): su//seng ge'i yid ni dga' bar 'gyur//seng ge grogs dang lhan cig tu//'di skad kyi ni tshig smras te//spyod pa bstan pa 'di la ni//bdag cag rjes su bslab par bgyi//de nas sangs 
4a1(dkon brtsegs): @#//rgyas 'dzum mdzad de//stong gsum po yang g.yos gyur nas//ma 'pham pas ni sangs rgyas la//ci zhig slad du 'dzum mdzad pa//thub chen lung bstan gsol zhes zhus//de skad zhu ba zhus 
4a2(dkon brtsegs): pa dang /ma 'pham pa la sangs rgyas kyis//'di skad ces ni bka' stsal to//gang gi phyir ni 'dzum mdzad pa//ma pham nga las 'dir nyon cig/sangs rgyas bye ba brgyad cu dang //gzhan yang khrag khrig phrag 
4a3(dkon brtsegs): cu la//byang chub don du 'dus nas ni//mi'i 'dren pa rnams la mchod//rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rkang gnyis mchog/mar me mdzad pa tshun chad du//bram ze'i khye'u 'di dag rnams//nga yis yongs su 
4a4(dkon brtsegs): smin par byas//snang ba che zhes bya ba yi//bskal pa mdzes po 'byung 'gyur te//de dag bskal pa gcig de la//rnam par 'dren par 'gyur ba ste//ye shes tog gi rgyal mtshan zhes//'jig 
4a5(dkon brtsegs): rten 'dren pa rnams su 'gyur//'od dpag med kyi bkod pa ltar//de dag gi yang de 'drar 'gyur//byams pa nas ni bzung nas su//bskal pa sum brgya tshang bar ni//de'i bar du dus kun 
4a6(dkon brtsegs): tu//rtag tu sangs rgyas phrad par 'gyur//'di dag byang chub thob pa yi//ming ni gang sus thos pa rnams//de dag thams cad 'jig rten du//kun mkhyen gang zag mtshungs med 'gyur//gang zhig lung 
4a7(dkon brtsegs): bstan 'di thos nas//mos pa skyed par byed pa ni//'jig rten gsum po 'di dag na//de dang mtshungs pa'i bsod nams med//gang gis bskal pa drug stong du//sangs rgyas zhing rnams thams cad 
4a8(dkon brtsegs): du//rin chen rnams kyis bkang byas nas//byang chub phyir 'dir byin pa bas//gang gis chos kyi rnam grangs 'di//thos nas byang chub phyir 'jug na//sngar smos pa'i bsod nams kyis//grangs dang 
4b1(dkon brtsegs): char yang mi phod do//chos 'di bshad pa'i tshe na ni//'jig rten mgon po'i mthu yis ni//lha rnams me tog char pa 'bebs//stong gsum po yang g.yos par gyur//seng ge grogs dang bcas pa yis//mngon par 
4b2(dkon brtsegs): shes pa lnga thob nas//nyin bdun bar du mchod byas nas//bstan pa la ni rab tu byung //de dag kun gyis ting 'dzin thob//zad mi shes pa'i gzungs kyang thob//sems can rnams kyi don byed de//sangs rgyas chos la

4b4(dkon brtsegs): yin dang //dri zar bcas pa'i 'jig rten yid rangs te//bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa la mngon par stod do//'dags pa dkon mchog brtsegs pa chen po'i chos kyi rnam grangs/le'u stong phrag brgya pa las/seng ge sa zhus pa zhes

4b6(dkon brtsegs): pa/shu lo ga drug cu'o//le'u sum bcu rtsa brgyad pa ste////@#//rgya gar skad du/Ar+ya sarba bud d+ha ma hA ra ha s+yo pA ya kau shal+ya dz+nyA no da ta bo d+hi satwa pa ri pr-its+tsha pi ri brtanA ma mahA yA na sU tra/bod skad du/'phags pa 
4b7(dkon brtsegs): sangs rgyas thams cad kyi gsang chen thabs la mkhas pa byang chub sems dpa' ye shes dam pas zhus pa'i le'u zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo/bam po dang /sangs rgyas dang byang chub 
4b8(dkon brtsegs): sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das mnyan yod na rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi tshal mgon mod zas sbyin gyi 
5a1(dkon brtsegs): @#//kun dga' ra ba na//slob pa dang mi slob pa'i dge slong brgyad stong gi dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po dang /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po khri drug stong thams cad kyang /mngon par shes 
5a2(dkon brtsegs): pa|gzungs thob pa/spobs pa thogs pa med pa/mngon par shes pa sgrub pa la mkhas pa/mi skye ba'i chos la bzod pa thob pa/yon tan 'bum phrag du ma dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sha stag dang thabs 
5a3(dkon brtsegs): gcig tu bzhugs te/de nas bcom ldan 'das phye ma red kyi dus kyi tshe nang du yang dag 'jog las bzhengs nas ring po ma lon par 'khor 'bum phrag du mas yongs su bskor cing mdun gyis bltas nas chos 
5a4(dkon brtsegs): ston te/tshangs par spyod pa/thog mar dge ba/bar du dge ba/tha mar dag ba/don bzang po/tshig 'bru bzang po/ma 'dres pa/yongs su rdzogs pa/yongs su dag ba/yongs su byang ba yang dag par ston to//
5a5(dkon brtsegs): de'i tshe na byang chub sems dpa' semasadapa' chen po ye shes dam pa zhes bya ba 'khor de nyid du 'dus te 'dug par gyur to//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po ye shes dmapa stan las langs te/
5a6(dkon brtsegs): bla gos phrag pa gcig tu bzar nas/bus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs te/bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de logs su thal mo sbyar ba btud nas/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//gal te 
5a7(dkon brtsegs): zhus nas zhu ba lung bstan pa'i slad du/bcom ldan 'das kyis bdag la skabs dbye bar gnang na/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas la bdag 
5a8(dkon brtsegs): phyogs 'ga' zhig zhu lags so//de skad ces gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' chen po ye shes dam ba la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//rigs kyi bu khyod ci 
5b1(dkon brtsegs): dang ci 'dod pa de bzhin gshegs pa la dris shig dang /ngas dris pa de dang /de nyid kyi lung bstan pas khyod kyi sems rangs par bya'o//de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang /bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems dpa' 
5b2(dkon brtsegs): sems dpa' chen po ye shes dam pas 'di skad ces gsol to//btsun pa bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyis thabs la mkhas pa thabs la mkhas pa zhes bgyi na/thabs 
5b3(dkon brtsegs): la mkhas pa de gang lags//btsun pa bcom ldan 'dis ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po thabs la mkhas pa lags//de skad ces gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'dis kyis byang chub sems dpa'
5b4(dkon brtsegs): sems dpa' chen po ye shas dam pa la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//rigs kyi bu legs so legs so//rigs kyi bu khyod skye bo mang po la phan pa dang /skye bo mang po la bde ba dang /'jig rten la snying brtse ba dang /lha rnams 
5b5(dkon brtsegs): dang /mi rnams kyi skye bo phal po che'i don dang /phan pa dang bde ba dang /da ltar dang /ma 'ongs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' semams dpa' chen po rnams gyi shes pa rjes su yongs su gzung ba dang /sangs rgyas kyi 
5b6(dkon brtsegs): chos thams cad yongs su rdzogs par bya ba dang /'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi dam pa'i chos yongs su gzung ba'i phyir zhugs te/rigs kyi bu khyod 
5b7(dkon brtsegs): de bzhin gshegs pa la byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi thabs la mkhas pa yongs su 'dri bar sems pa ni rigs kyi bu yang khyod legs so//rigs kyi bu de'i phyir legs par rab tu nyon la tshul bzhin du yid la zung 
5b8(dkon brtsegs): shig dang /ngas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi thabs la mkhas ba khyod la bshad par bya'o//byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po ye shes dam pas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin 
6a1(dkon brtsegs): @#//
6b1(dkon brtsegs): ljon pa dang /spos kyi shing ljon pa dang /me tog dang /me tog 'phreng dang /spos dang /phye ma dang /byug pa bdag gir ma byas shing /yongs su ma bzung ba gang ji snyed pa de dag thams cad bsam pas yongs su bzung zhing 
6b2(dkon brtsegs): yongs su bsdus nas/sangs rgyas thams cad la 'bul bar byed cing /dge ba'i rtsa ba de yang sems can thams cad la gtong bar byed de/yongs su btang nas kyang thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du yongs su sngo bar 
6b3(dkon brtsegs): byed do//phyogs bcu dag na me tog gi shing ljon pa dang /spos kyis shing ljon pa dang /me tig dang me tog 'phreng dang /spo dang /phye ma dang /phyug pa bdag gi dang bcas shing yongs su bzung ba dang bcas pa gang ji snyed pa de dag
6b4(dkon brtsegs): thams cad kyi dri rlung gis btab pa'i dang de dag kyang byang chub sems dpa' thabs la mkhas pas sangs rgyas thams cad la 'phul bar byed cing /dge pa'i rtsa ba de yang bdag dang /sems can thams cad kyis thams 
6b5(dkon brtsegs): cad mkhyen pa nyid yongs su rdzogs par bya ba'i phyir yongs su sngo bar byed do//de thams cad mkhyen pa nyid do yongs su bsngos pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba des/tshul khrims dang /ting nge 'dzin dang /shes rab dang /rnam 
6b6(dkon brtsegs): par grol ba dang /rnam par grol ba'i ye shes mthong ba'i phung po dpag tu med pa thob par 'gyur te/rigs kyi bu de yang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po'i thabs la mkhas pa yin no//rigs kyi bu 
6b7(dkon brtsegs): gzhan yang byang chub sems dpa'i sems dpa' chen po thabs la mkhas pa ni phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams dag na sems can sdug bsngal gyi tshor ba myong bar gyur pa gang ji snyed pa de dag la/byang chub sems dpa' 
6b8(dkon brtsegs): thabs la mkhas pas sems can de dag gi sdug bsngal gyi tshor ba de dag thams cad bsdus te 'chags par byed cing /'di ltar sems can de dag gi sdug/bsngal gyi tshor ba gang dag yin pa de dag thams cad bdag gi lus 
7a1(dkon brtsegs): @#//la 'bab par 'gyur zhing /sems can de dag bde bar gyur cig ces go cha gyon par yang byed do//phyogs bcu kun gyi 'jig rten gyi khams dag na/sems can bde ba'i tshor ba myong bar gyur pa gang dag yin pa 
7a2(dkon brtsegs): de dag la/byang chub sems dpa' thabs la mkhas pa de dag yang dag par bsdus te/rjes su yi rang bar byed cing /rjes su yi rang nas kyang /thams cad mkhyen pa nyid la dmigs pa'i sems bskyed pas/sems can 
7a3(dkon brtsegs): thams cad kyang sdug bsngal thams cad rab tu zhi bar bya ba'i phyir thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du yongs su sngo bar byed do//de dge ba'i rtsa ba des sangs rgyas thams cad kyi bde ba'i yo byad rnams 'thob par 'gyur te/rigs kyi 
7a4(dkon brtsegs): bu de yang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po'i thabs la mkhas pa yin no//rigs kyi bu gzhan yang byang chub sems dpa'i sems dpa' chen po thabs la mkhas pa ni de bzhin gshegs pa gcig la phyag byas 
7a5(dkon brtsegs): bkur sti byas/bla mar byas/ri mo byas/mchod pa byas/bsnyen bkur byas nas/'di ltar so sor rtog par byed de/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni chos kyi dbyings gcig las nges par byung ba dang /
7a6(dkon brtsegs): tshul khrims gcig pa dang /ting nge 'dzin gcig pa dang /shes rab gcig pa dang /rnam par grol ba gcig pa dang /rnam pa grol ba ye shes mthong ba gcig pa dang /ye shes gcig pa dang /rig pa gcig 
7a7(dkon brtsegs): pa yin no zhes so sor rnam par rtog par byed do//des de ltar so sor brtags nas 'di ltar shes par byed de/de bzhin gshegs pa gcig pa la phyag byas/bkur sti byas/bla mar bya ba/ri mo byas/mchod pa las/
7a8(dkon brtsegs): bsnyen bkur byas pas ni de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad la phyag byas pa dang /bkur sti byas pa dang /bla mar byas pa dang /ri mor byas pa dang /mchod pa byas pa dang /bsnyen bkur byas par 'gyur ro snyam du shes 
7b1(dkon brtsegs): par byed do//byang chub sems dpa' thabs la mkhas pa ni phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag gi mchod pa 'byung bar 'gyur ba gang dag yin pa de dag thams cad bsam pas yongs su bzung nas/
7b2(dkon brtsegs): thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du yongs su sngo bar byed de/rigs kyi bu de yang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po'i thabs la mkhas pa yin no//rigs kyi bu gzhan yang byang chub sems dpa'i sems 
7b3(dkon brtsegs): dpa' chen po thabs la mkhas pa ni/gang gi tshe blo rtul bar gyur na yang de bdag nyid khyad du gsod par mi byed cing /zhum par mi byed par/de tha na tshig bzhi pa'i tshigs su bcad pa gcig tsam la yang 'jug 
7b4(dkon brtsegs): par byed cing /de 'di ltar so sor rtog par yang byed de/tshig bzhi pa'i tshigs su bcang pa gcig po 'di'i don gang yin pa de ni/gsung rab tu rtogs pa thams cad kyi don yin no zhes so sor rtog par byed do//des 
7b5(dkon brtsegs): de ltar tshig bzhi pa'i tshigs su btsad pa gcig kha ton du bslabs nas/zhum pa med pa'i sems kyis snying rje chen po bskyed de/rnyed pa dang /bkur sti dang /grags pa 'dod pa med pas grong dang /grong khyer dang /
7b6(dkon brtsegs): grong rdal dang /yul 'khor dang /rgyal po'i pho brang 'khor dag tu skye bo mang po la tshig bzhi pa'i tshigs su brtsad pa de rgya cher yang dag par ston par byed cing /'di ltar smon lam 'debs par yang byed de/sems can gang 
7b7(dkon brtsegs): su dag gis bdag gi tshig bzhi pa'i tshigs su bcid pa 'di thos par gyur pa de dag thams cad bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu nges par gyur cig ces smon lam 'debs par yangabyed do//de'i dge 
7b8(dkon brtsegs): ba'i rtsa ba'i thabs la mkhas pas yongs su zin pa des sems can thams cad kun dga' bo ltar mang du thos par 'gyur zhing /sangs rgyas kyi spobs pa nyid kyang 'thob par 'gyur te/rigs kyi bu de yang

8a2(dkon brtsegs): dbul por gyur na yang des tha na gzhan gyi las kyang byas te/kha zas nal ze gang tsam yang blangs nas/zhum pa med pa'i sems kyis dge 'dun nam gang zag la 'bul bar yang byed do//phul nas kyang de 'di ltar

8a7(dkon brtsegs): chub kyi sems dang bral ba'i sems can rnams kyi sbyin pa las byung ba'i bsod nams bya ba'i dngos po yang zil gyis gnon par 'gyur la skyes bu chen po'i mtshan ro gro ba'i mchog kyang 'grub par 'gyur ro//

8b1(dkon brtsegs): chen po thabs la mkhas pa ni gal te nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas rnams dang /nyan thos kyi theg pa pa rnams dad rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pa pa rnams dang /lhan cig gnas par gyur kyang /de dag la dga' 
8b2(dkon brtsegs): bar mi byed cing /gal te byang chub sems dpa' thabs la mkhas pas nyan thos dang /rang sang rgyas rnams la bsnyen bkur lhag pa dag yod la bdag la de lta ma yin par mthong na yang /de rgyu gnyis 
8b3(dkon brtsegs): kyis bdag la rtog par byed de/gnyis gang zhe na/byang chub sems dpa' las nges par byung ba ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams yin la/sangs rgyas bcom ldan

8b5(dkon brtsegs): bdag nyid shin tu mchog dang /shin tu gtso bo yin gyi/'di dag ni ma yin no//'di dag ni bdag gi pha'i nor gyi phung po la yongs su spyod par byed pa ma yin pas/bdag gis 'di dag la dga' bar yang mi

8b7(dkon brtsegs): 'gyur la/bsnyen bkur ma byas kyang zhum par mi 'gyur zhing /sems can thams cad la thugs mnyam pa kun tu 'dzin par mdzad pa lta bu'i rjes su chags pa dang /khong khro ba spangs pa 'thob par

9a1(dkon brtsegs): @#//sbyin pa sbyin pas kyang pha rol tu phyin pa drug po thams cad yongs su rdzogs par byed do//ji ltar yongs su rdzogs par byed ce na/'di la byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po thabs

9a3(dkon brtsegs): kyang skyed par byed pa gang yin pa de ni de'i sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa yin no//tshul khrims yang dag par blangs shing /tshul khrims dang ldan pa la sbyin par byed pa dang /sems can tshul khrims 'chal ba 
9a4(dkon brtsegs): gang dag yin pa de dag tshul khrims la 'god par byed cing sbyin pa byin nas 'di ltar bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub la 'god par byed pa gang yin pa/de ni de'i tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu 
9a5(dkon brtsegs): phyin pa yin no//byams pa'i sems dang /phan pa'i sems dang /ma 'dres pa'i sems dang /ma 'khrugs pa'i sems dang /le lo med pa'i sems dang /rnyog pa med pa'i sems dang /snyoms pa'i

9a8(dkon brtsegs): pha rol tu phyin pa yin no//byin pa gang yin pa de la sems rtse gcig pa nyid dang /dga' ba dang /yid bde ba dang /mchog tu dga' ba'i bsam pa dang /rnam par g.yeng ba med pa gang yin pa de ni 
9b1(dkon brtsegs): de'i bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa yin no//sbyin pa byin nas chos nyid kyi rjes su yang dag par gzhol bar byed de/su sa byin su la byin/ji zhig byin/'di'i rnam par smin pa sus myong bar 'gyur zhes chos

9b3(dkon brtsegs): chos 'ga' yang mi dmigs pa gang yin pa de ni de'i shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yin to/rigs kyi bu de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po thabs la mkhas pa ni sbyin pa sbyin pa bas kyang pha rol 
9b4(dkon brtsegs): tu phyin pa drug po thams cad yongs su rdzogs par byed pa yin no//de nas bcom ldan 'das la byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po ye shes dam pas 'di skad ces gsol to//btsun pa bcom 
9b5(dkon brtsegs): ldan 'das de ltar byang chub sems dpa' semams dpa' chen po thabs las mkhas pas sbyin pa gang gis sems can rnams kyang 'khor ba las yongs su 'dzin par bgyid la/sbyin pa de nyid kyis sangs rgyas kyi chos 
9b6(dkon brtsegs): thams cad kyang yongs su 'dzin par bgyis pa'i byang chub sems dpa' semag dpa' chen po rnams kyi thabs la mkhas pa de ni ngo mtshar lags so//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu de de bzhin
